Γöé  escalation tier. Rules are evaluated from highest to lowest severity; the  Γöé
Γöé  highest matching tier wins.                                                Γöé
Γöé  EMERGENCY triggers (any ONE is sufficient): - Symptom name contains any    Γöé
Γöé  of: "faint", "syncope", "lost consciousness",                              Γöé
Γöé    "blacked out", "chest pain", "shortness of breath at rest",              Γöé

Γöé                                                                             Γöé
Γöé  URGENT triggers (any ONE is sufficient, only if EMERGENCY not already      Γöé
Γöé  triggered): - life_stage is "menopausal" (or "postmenopausal") AND any     Γöé
Γöé  tagged symptom                                                             Γöé
Γöé    with clinical_flag == "red_flag" or severity in ["moderate", "severe"]   Γöé
Γöé  - life_stage is "pregnant" (or is_pregnant == true) AND a tagged symptom   Γöé
Γöé    whose name is "fatigue" or "dizziness"/"dizzy" with severity "severe"    Γöé
Γöé  - Any tagged symptom with name containing "palpitation", "racing heart",   Γöé
Γöé    "heart racing", "tachycardia" together with any symptom containing       Γöé
Γöé    "dizzy", "dizziness", "lightheaded"                                      Γöé
Γöé  - Any tagged symptom with name/raw_text containing "weight loss" with      Γöé
Γöé    severity in ["moderate", "severe"] OR quantity >= 5% of body weight in   Γöé
Γöé  a month (if body_weight_kg is provided in patient input). If               Γöé
Γöé  body_weight_kg is NOT provided, use >= 3kg/month as the fallback flat      Γöé
Γöé  threshold.                                                                 Γöé
Γöé  - Three or more tagged symptoms with severity == "moderate" (regardless    Γöé
Γöé  of life stage) - prior_monitor_flags_last_window == true AND the current   Γöé
Γöé  tagged symptoms contain                                                    Γöé
Γöé    the SAME symptom name/pattern that caused the prior MONITOR trigger      Γöé
Γöé  (not just any new unrelated symptom)                                       Γöé
Γöé                                                                             Γöé

Γöé  - Any tagged symptom with clinical_flag == "watch" and no higher-tier      Γöé
Γöé  rules fire                                                                 Γöé

Γöé  Task: You will receive the structured JSON output from the symptom         Γöé
Γöé  tagging task. Apply the following safety rule hierarchy to determine the   Γöé
Γöé  escalation tier. Rules are evaluated from highest to lowest severity; the  Γöé
Γöé  highest matching tier wins.                                                Γöé
Γöé  EMERGENCY triggers (any ONE is sufficient): - Symptom name contains any    Γöé
Γöé  of: "faint", "syncope", "lost consciousness",                              Γöé
Γöé    "blacked out", "chest pain", "shortness of breath at rest",              Γöé
Γöé    "out of breath sitting", "black stool", "tarry stool", "melena",         Γöé
Γöé    "soaking through protection every hour", "soaking pad", "heavy bleeding  Γöé
Γöé  hourly"                                                                    Γöé
Γöé                                                                             Γöé
Γöé  URGENT triggers (any ONE is sufficient, only if EMERGENCY not already      Γöé
Γöé  triggered): - life_stage is "menopausal" (or "postmenopausal") AND any     Γöé
Γöé  tagged symptom                                                             Γöé
Γöé    with clinical_flag == "red_flag" or severity in ["moderate", "severe"]   Γöé
Γöé  - life_stage is "pregnant" (or is_pregnant == true) AND a tagged symptom   Γöé
Γöé    whose name is "fatigue" or "dizziness"/"dizzy" with severity "severe"    Γöé
Γöé  - Any tagged symptom with name containing "palpitation", "racing heart",   Γöé
Γöé    "heart racing", "tachycardia" together with any symptom containing       Γöé
Γöé    "dizzy", "dizziness", "lightheaded"                                      Γöé
Γöé  - Any tagged symptom with name/raw_text containing "weight loss" with      Γöé
Γöé    severity in ["moderate", "severe"] OR quantity >= 5% of body weight in   Γöé
Γöé  a month (if body_weight_kg is provided in patient input). If               Γöé
Γöé  body_weight_kg is NOT provided, use >= 3kg/month as the fallback flat      Γöé
Γöé  threshold.                                                                 Γöé
Γöé  - Three or more tagged symptoms with severity == "moderate" (regardless    Γöé
Γöé  of life stage) - prior_monitor_flags_last_window == true AND the current   Γöé
Γöé  tagged symptoms contain                                                    Γöé
Γöé    the SAME symptom name/pattern that caused the prior MONITOR trigger      Γöé
Γöé  (not just any new unrelated symptom)                                       Γöé
Γöé                                                                             Γöé
Γöé  MONITOR triggers (any ONE is sufficient, only if EMERGENCY and URGENT not  Γöé
Γöé  already triggered): - biomarkers contains MCV with flag == "low" AND       Γöé
Γöé  family_history_flags                                                       Γöé
Γöé    contains "thalassemia" or "haemoglobinopathy"                            Γöé
Γöé  - Any tagged symptom with clinical_flag == "watch" and no higher-tier      Γöé
Γöé  rules fire                                                                 Γöé

Γöé                                                                             Γöé
Γöé  Task: You will receive the structured JSON output from the symptom         Γöé
Γöé  tagging task. Apply the following safety rule hierarchy to determine the   Γöé
Γöé  escalation tier. Rules are evaluated from highest to lowest severity; the  Γöé
Γöé  highest matching tier wins.                                                Γöé
Γöé  EMERGENCY triggers (any ONE is sufficient): - Symptom name contains any    Γöé
Γöé  of: "faint", "syncope", "lost consciousness",                              Γöé
Γöé    "blacked out", "chest pain", "shortness of breath at rest",              Γöé
Γöé    "out of breath sitting", "black stool", "tarry stool", "melena",         Γöé
Γöé    "soaking through protection every hour", "soaking pad", "heavy bleeding  Γöé
Γöé  hourly"                                                                    Γöé
Γöé                                                                             Γöé
Γöé  URGENT triggers (any ONE is sufficient, only if EMERGENCY not already      Γöé
Γöé  triggered): - life_stage is "menopausal" (or "postmenopausal") AND any     Γöé
Γöé  tagged symptom                                                             Γöé
Γöé    with clinical_flag == "red_flag" or severity in ["moderate", "severe"]   Γöé
Γöé  - life_stage is "pregnant" (or is_pregnant == true) AND a tagged symptom   Γöé
Γöé    whose name is "fatigue" or "dizziness"/"dizzy" with severity "severe"    Γöé
Γöé  - Any tagged symptom with name containing "palpitation", "racing heart",   Γöé
Γöé    "heart racing", "tachycardia" together with any symptom containing       Γöé
Γöé    "dizzy", "dizziness", "lightheaded"                                      Γöé
Γöé  - Any tagged symptom with name/raw_text containing "weight loss" with      Γöé
Γöé    severity in ["moderate", "severe"] OR quantity >= 5% of body weight in   Γöé
Γöé  a month (if body_weight_kg is provided in patient input). If               Γöé
Γöé  body_weight_kg is NOT provided, use >= 3kg/month as the fallback flat      Γöé
Γöé  threshold.                                                                 Γöé
Γöé  - Three or more tagged symptoms with severity == "moderate" (regardless    Γöé
Γöé  of life stage) - prior_monitor_flags_last_window == true AND the current   Γöé
Γöé  tagged symptoms contain                                                    Γöé
Γöé    the SAME symptom name/pattern that caused the prior MONITOR trigger      Γöé
Γöé  (not just any new unrelated symptom)                                       Γöé
Γöé                                                                             Γöé
Γöé  MONITOR triggers (any ONE is sufficient, only if EMERGENCY and URGENT not  Γöé
Γöé  already triggered): - biomarkers contains MCV with flag == "low" AND       Γöé
Γöé  family_history_flags                                                       Γöé
Γöé    contains "thalassemia" or "haemoglobinopathy"                            Γöé
Γöé  - Any tagged symptom with clinical_flag == "watch" and no higher-tier      Γöé
Γöé  rules fire                                                                 Γöé
Γöé  NONE: - No rules from EMERGENCY, URGENT, or MONITOR fired - OR             Γöé
Γöé  tagged_symptoms list is empty                                              Γöé

Γöé  tier: the determined tier (EMERGENCY | URGENT | MONITOR | NONE) -          Γöé
Γöé  rules_fired: list of rule descriptions that were triggered - rationale: a  Γöé
Γöé  brief, plain-language explanation for the patient - tagged_symptoms: pass  Γöé
Γöé  through from input (for audit logging) - life_stage_context: pass through  Γöé
Γöé  from input                                                                 Γöé
Γöé  Output ONLY a valid JSON object ΓÇö no markdown, no explanation, no          Γöé
Γöé  preamble.                                                                  Γöé
